The shared ordered ClassElement List and retained constructor environment provide formal/actual arguments, element order, inner names, super, return completions and actual newTarget across these source records. Compact Node differential properties cover the semantic partitions with both memory modes and one deep inheritance/owner graph; global_class_definition checks JavaScript exports imported by typed consumers. TypeScript native class optimization, private elements, suspended heritage/member keys, eval environments and eligible full target coverage remain open.

Dynamic class heritage and derived construction now use the canonical ClassElement List, actual argument List and retained Function Environment Record. Heritage validates a live constructor/prototype or null and establishes both prototype links before member keys. Symbol now shares the ordinary Realm Reference, callable owner, argument List and actual-result path with the other primitive constructors. Object.prototype.toString first boxes primitives and determines the builtin brand, then performs ordinary @@toStringTag Get; BigInt/Symbol fallback to Object when their tags are absent or non-strings, RegExp uses its internal slot, and a tag getter cannot retroactively change the precomputed proxy brand. Complete array exotic and iterator semantics, ordinary platform registration, general local class/HomeObject/private/super semantics, async Module evaluation and reciprocal/signed full coverage remain open.

Source ambient declarations now borrow one declaration-owner predicate with library declarations. An ancestor worklist recognizes explicit declare contexts without creating runtime bindings; global-name admission, variable emission, capture cells, native-result facts and native constructor routing use that same distinction. Unknown ambient owners fail closed with the stable ordinary-owner diagnostic. `tests/property/ambient-bindings.property.test.ts` compares erased Node strict/sloppy intrinsic construction, callbacks, real shadows, suspension and one deep retained binding tree through typed/JavaScript CLI and observation profiles in both memory modes; native host adapters are checked in typed CLI profiles because the observation and erased-JavaScript host profiles do not install those platform globals. `ambient_native_bindings` verifies imported source owners and native construction in both memory modes; `ambient_unknown_owner_diagnostic` checks unsupported ownership. Array.fromAsync now projects through one ordinary Realm-owned callable and one retained runtime phase worklist. The shared Iterator Record captures async or sync methods once; the existing async-from-sync owner awaits only sync values. Every Await queues a continuation, including fulfilled inputs, and pending promises resume the same rooted source/mapper/receiver/result/completion cells. Array-like sources snapshot ToLength and await each Get before mapping; iterable sources retain next-result/done/value ordering without preconsumption. Generic construction, indexed CreateDataProperty and final Set length borrow the ordinary Array factory operations. Mapper and indexed-definition failures close the iterator with incoming throw precedence; next/done/value and final-length failures propagate directly. Continuations restore their saved Realm and Environment. The eager emitter loop and fresh-pending placeholder are deleted. `tests/property/array-from-async.property.test.ts` compares independent JavaScript transcriptions of the pinned algorithms through typed/JavaScript CLI and observation profiles in both memory modes, including value kinds, actual callback Lists, method mutation, deferred resolution, constructor/descriptor ordering, close errors, Promise constructor failures and one deep thenable chain. Node 26.11's built-in reads a sync next getter twice and retries closing after next/return rejection, so it is unsuitable as the complete protocol oracle. `array_from_async_protocol` verifies imported callable owners, pending jobs and native array projections in both memory modes. The committed predecessor emits no result for the deferred-input probe; the shared worklist emits `pending: true true`. Pinned harness/language admission gaps, complete Realm and native platform contracts, async Module evaluation, private/super semantics and reciprocal/signed full coverage remain open.

Ordinary typed source function declarations, expressions and arrows now enter the same actual-value ABI, callable activation and formal BindingInitialization tree as JavaScript functions and object members. Source parameter syntax defines the physical slots and rest adapter; type annotations and generic instantiation do not coerce ordinary actual values or returned values. Parameter expressions retain their evaluated defaults, TDZ cells, actual arguments mapping, lexical arrow captures and separate parameter/body bindings across ordinary, async and generator entry. The lifted-arrow, zero-argument inline-call and private callback body/arity selectors are deleted. One actual argument List builder serves source calls, native array/collection/comparator callbacks, decorators and native callback adapters. One queued callback owner retains a rooted callable and actual List rather than per-formal fields. Callback element snapshots retain the original value through mutation/collection; predicate and comparator conversion occurs at the consumer boundary. Formal storage projection no longer changes the physical type of calls inside default expressions. Native Event, EventTarget and EventEmitter payloads borrow stable object owners, live Event getters and a method registry for represented event callbacks. `tests/property/source-callable-formals.property.test.ts` compares independent Node strict/sloppy value/default/arguments/TDZ/body-scope/suspension/native-callback/generic partitions, forced collection and one deep formal tree through typed/JavaScript source in both memory modes; `source_callable_formals` verifies imported callable owners, physical this adapters and retained queued callbacks in both memory modes. Native class formals, complete platform metadata/branding and event interoperability, complete async Array.fromAsync interleaving/continuations/IteratorClose, HomeObject/super/private semantics, async Module evaluation and reciprocal/signed full coverage remain open.

Ordinary typed object-literal methods and accessors now consume the same source formal collection and BindingInitialization tree as JavaScript callables. One semantic owner predicate chooses actual value slots, rest Lists, returned values and captured value/root/TDZ cells; the existing source fact graph carries that convention across inferred owners, aliases and body var copies. Evaluated defaults, nested iterator/object patterns, computed Symbol exclusions and retained parameter closures no longer enter the private literal/shape parameter path. Strict, simple sloppy-mapped and non-simple unmapped arguments share the actual activation, including absent actuals. Parameter expressions preserve separate parameter/body bindings through ordinary, async and generator entry, while simple var redeclarations share the binding. Canonical JavaScript callable type projection also derives from source syntax, preventing recursive checker signatures or differing inferred result codecs from changing the physical ABI. One symbol-owned global root plan now serves early function writes and later module initialization, so boxed replacement strings survive forced collection independently of emission order. Explicit native this receivers keep their physical adapter. `tests/property/object-member-formals.property.test.ts` compares independent Node value/default/TDZ/key/iterator-close/arguments/body-scope/abrupt partitions, forced collection and one deep formal tree through typed/JavaScript profiles in both memory modes; `object_member_formals` verifies typed source modules, retained scope cells and suspended values in both memory modes. The original evaluated-default probe fails admission, and the body-scope probe returns 17/17 before repair versus 17/11 afterward. Wider typed ordinary-function/native class/callback formal conventions, complete HomeObject/super and object metadata, async Module evaluation and reciprocal/signed full coverage remain open. The final pinned unmapped-arguments and later-formal selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-member89-pinned-arguments-final.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-member89-pinned-tdz-final.json`) pass natively for their exact strict/sloppy inventories, with unchanged source fingerprints and audited runner-owned C/executable manifests. Their exact metadata maps DefineMethod, rather than FunctionDeclarationInstantiation or a whole-clause result; the diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Ordinary typed object-literal methods, getters and setters now borrow one shared callable activation predicate with JavaScript functions. Callable type mapping and boxing retain the actual receiver, strict/sloppy ThisMode, actual argument List and lexical arrow captures through async/generator activation. Receiver-dependent inferred results enter the existing source value fact worklist, preserving borrowed receiver identity without a native scalar/struct result conversion; async generators keep their generator result ABI. Explicit typed this conventions remain physical native adapters. One native-type forward collection now covers named declarations and callable receiver/parameter/result dependencies and emits before every generated struct, replacing source-order forward emission. `tests/property/object-member-activation.property.test.ts` compares independent Node receiver-kind, borrowed/bound/accessor, non-constructability, argument/arrow, suspension and inferred-result partitions, forced collection and one deep retained owner tree through typed/JavaScript profiles in both memory modes; `object_member_activation` verifies imported typed owners and explicit native this dependencies in both memory modes. Broader typed ordinary-function/formal/mapped-argument conventions, complete object metadata/HomeObject-super semantics, class/private activation, async Module evaluation and full reciprocal/signed coverage remain open. The pinned non-strict method selection (`/tmp/tsc2c-object88-pinned-this.json`) passes natively with its exact scenario inventory, unchanged source fingerprints and audited runner-owned C/executable manifests. Its legacy `es6id: 14.3.8` metadata has no current exact clause mapping; it remains diagnostic evidence on an ineligible host.

Suspended destructuring assignments now enter the same canonical BindingName tree as declarations and iterator heads. The resumable operation worklist retains the RHS for result identity, saved Reference operands, defaults and normalized source property keys; local, outer and Module identifier writes borrow the ordinary Reference dispatcher. Array patterns borrow the loop Iterator Record collection, including holes, rest consumption and nested normal/throw/injected-return close edges; object rest borrows shared CopyDataProperties with string and Symbol exclusions. The indexed pattern-read/rest and direct CFG write predecessors are deleted. Binding value slots preserve actual values, including void/never-inferred awaited defaults, while native Reference operands retain their physical storage. Computed member names resolve in their surrounding source activation. `tests/property/suspending-binding-tree.property.test.ts` compares independent Node evaluation/default/Reference/key/close partitions, actual value kinds, generator completion injection, forced collection and one deep suspended tree through typed/JavaScript native profiles in both memory modes; `suspending_binding_tree` verifies imported typed native field/accessor/index targets and retained source identity in both memory modes. Complete saved Environment/with/eval References, typed object-method activation, wider native callable/immutable-update conventions, async Module evaluation, reciprocal clause registration and signed host/report eligibility remain open. The final pinned yielded-default and injected-return close-error selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-assignment87-pinned-yield-final.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-assignment87-pinned-close-final.json`) pass natively for their exact strict/sloppy inventories, with unchanged source fingerprints and audited runner-owned C/executable manifests. Ordinary destructuring assignments now project through the same BindingName tree and initialization algorithm as declarations. One shared normalizer retains property-reference leaves for ordinary and resumable iterator consumers, replacing the private iterator predecessor. Leaf preparation saves rooted receiver/key operands before iterator steps or property Get/default operations; PutValue uses the ordinary assignment dispatcher, with source result identity, nested/rest/empty patterns, symbol exclusions, strict write checks and abrupt IteratorClose retained. Native top-level constant simple writes now preserve actual RHS evaluation and TDZ/error precedence instead of emitting an invalid C store. `tests/property/assignment-binding-tree.property.test.ts` compares independent Node Reference/step/Get/default/put/close partitions, actual native replacement values, suspension boundaries and one deep assignment tree through typed/JavaScript native profiles in both memory modes; `assignment_binding_tree` verifies imported typed field/accessor/index targets in both memory modes. The async iterator regression now checks canonical Iterator Record storage; its former storage-name expectations also fail against the pre-change committed compiler. Pattern-internal suspension, complete saved Environment/with/eval references, typed object-method activation, wider native callable/immutable-update conventions and full language/host coverage remain open. The pinned default-order and object-rest selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-assignment86-pinned-default.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-assignment86-pinned-rest.json`) pass natively for their complete strict/sloppy inventories, with unchanged source fingerprints and validated runner-attested C/executable manifests. Their generated metadata retains the exact VariableStatement parent clause (plus Symbol clauses for rest), rather than exact DestructuringAssignmentEvaluation ownership; reciprocal registration and diagnostic-host claim eligibility remain open.

Typed Module binding patterns now enter the existing Module BoundNames cell collection and shared BindingInitialization tree. Pattern source/default aggregates use the canonical boxed Array/Object producers, including nested and conditional literals, while module cell reads retain actual values rather than converting checker scalar types before observation. This preserves var-before-initialization, lexical TDZ, iterator/Get/default/rest/close ordering and live imports/namespace reads. `tests/property/typed-module-binding-tree.property.test.ts` compares independently erased Node modules across cyclic instantiation, declaration kinds, nested typed/default sources, actual iterator value kinds, abrupt close and one deep binding tree through both native profiles and memory modes; `typed_module_binding_tree` checks typed module E2E in both memory modes. Ordinary native source facts now include actual argument and later-write inputs. One source-tree worklist adds formal-slot, callback/helper, alias-owner, binding-pattern, computed-field and mutating receiver edges to the same cached fixed point; value producers share their owners' write domain. The scoped ABI projection follows source signatures for direct, retained and callback result adapters and chooses one boxed codec for affected parameters, returns, bindings, fields and collections even when scalar annotations are present. Function result facts still come only from returned expressions, so unrelated effects do not narrow or redefine their result convention. `tests/property/ordinary-native-source-flow.property.test.ts` now checks independent Node Type/identity/effect partitions across annotated conventions, binding/field/index writes, alias codecs, binding patterns, computed field selection, rest/spread, callbacks and call/apply/bind/Reflect paths, with forced collection and the same deep source dependency in both native profiles and memory modes. `ordinary_native_slot_flow` checks these paths through typed source modules in both memory modes. The pre-change probe (`/tmp/tsc2c-result84-baseline-run.log`) loses each replacement value; the repaired probe preserves them. Assignment-pattern and module-scope pattern lowering still fail closed and require the canonical reference/binding path. Complete callable escape/overload/generic domains, arbitrary native owner categories, ordinary collection method semantics, complete class activation and full language/host coverage remain open.

Ordinary Date/Buffer results now retain their actual values across inferred source ABI slots. The existing cached dependency graph follows source call targets, returned expressions, alias initializers and field owners; function bodies contribute their return values rather than unrelated effects. One emitter-scoped type projection supplies the same boxed result convention to declarations, callable adapters, calls, fields, aggregates and awaited results. Native and boxed property reads consume the same facts, and inferred array/record producers use the canonical value codec. `tests/property/ordinary-native-source-flow.property.test.ts` compares independent Node Type/identity/effect partitions, forced collection and one deep source-call dependency through both native profiles and memory modes; `ordinary_native_source_flow` verifies imports, retained closures, fields/getters, aggregate results and suspension in both memory modes. The pre-change probe (`/tmp/tsc2c-result83-baseline-run.log`) loses replacement identity across these ABI boundaries, and the repaired probe preserves it. Explicit annotation/parameter conventions, later binding/field/collection writes, complete callable inference and wider language/host coverage remain open.

Typed Date calls, construction and constructor-value reads now use ordinary Environment Record lookup and the shared rooted callee/actual-List application. One native-owner descriptor collection governs instance members, constructor globals and inferred constructor/call results; the existing fact worklist preserves actual values through aliases, conditionals and named/computed constructor references. Unused invocations and typeof observe replacement, errors and missing bindings. The direct Date call/new lowering, constructor/fresh-operand purity paths and Date typeof constant predecessor are deleted. Runtime Date computes its clipped DateValue before GetPrototypeFromConstructor and allocation, using the common exotic-allocation policy and a retained date root across prototype getters. `tests/property/date-entry-invocation.property.test.ts` compares independent Node lookup/Type/identity/coercion/missing-target/prototype-order partitions and one deep retained actual-List worklist through both native profiles and memory modes. `date_entry_invocation` verifies typed aliases, unused effects, conversion order and captured construction through suspension in both memory modes. General callable return/field/aggregate inference, complete class/heritage activation, named-zone semantics and reciprocal/signed coverage remain open. The pinned conversion-order and foreign-realm prototype selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-date82-pinned-coercion.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date82-pinned-prototype.json`) pass natively with unchanged source fingerprints, exact Date-constructor clause mapping and runner-attested C/executable manifests. The diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Date.parse and string construction now project one grammar traversal into a shared semantic calendar-field record. Ordered field descriptors carry defaults, prerequisites, widths and ranges; signed expanded years reject negative zero, partial date/time forms preserve omission, absent offsets select UTC for date-only text and local time for date-time text, and 24:00 advances through MakeTime. Runtime UTC/local text projections use the same record, named month/weekday collections and common Number MakeDay/MakeTime/MakeDate lowering, with TimeClip after offset conversion. The fixed complete-ISO parser and C tm/timegm normalization predecessor are deleted. Existing lowercase separators and arbitrary fractional digit runs remain explicit extensions of the same record; other unrecognized text, including embedded NUL, returns NaN. `tests/property/date-text-parse.property.test.ts` compares shared ISO grammar/offset/clip/coercion partitions with Node, verifies negative civil-year runtime roundtrips against independent millisecond values where Node rejects them, and retains one deep text-owner worklist through both native profiles and memory modes. `date_text_parse` verifies typed parsing and producer roundtrips in both memory modes. Complete named-zone transition/disambiguation semantics, formal implementation-choice/reciprocal registrations and full signed host/report eligibility remain open. The complete pinned parse directory and the formerly failing exact UTC/local civil negative-year selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-date81-pinned-parse.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date81-pinned-civil.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date81-pinned-local.json`) now pass natively with unchanged source fingerprints and runner-attested C/executable manifests. Expanded-year negative-zero evidence keeps its exact expanded-year clause; the generic parse non-constructability test still retains parent/reflection/arrow metadata without the exact Date.parse owner, so reciprocal registration stays open. The diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Date text formatting now shares one sign/magnitude year projection. ISO output uses four unsigned digits for years 0 through 9999 and signed six-digit expanded years outside that interval; UTC and local civil text retain a separate negative sign and an absolute year padded to at least four digits. The direct signed `%04d` predecessors are removed from ISO/UTC/date/full string formatters. `tests/property/date-year-text.property.test.ts` compares independent sign/padding partitions and Node observations across the TimeClip domain, UTC/fixed offsets and one deep retained owner tree through CLI/native observation in both memory modes; `date_year_text` checks typed consumers in both memory modes. Parsing, complete named-zone transitions, locale text choices, remaining reciprocal Date clauses and signed host/report eligibility remain open. The complete pinned ISO directory selection (`/tmp/tsc2c-date80-pinned-iso.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints and runner-attested C/executable manifests. Its generic non-constructability metadata retains parent/reflection/arrow clauses and still lacks the exact Date ISO owner mapping; full reciprocal registration remains open. The exact civil negative-year selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-date80-pinned-civil.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date80-pinned-local.json`) compile and execute natively but fail their assertions because signed expanded-year strings are rejected by the existing parser. The pre-parser-change native probe (`/tmp/tsc2c-date81-parse-baseline-run.log`) independently reproduces expanded/partial/date-time/midnight and runtime-UTC-text parsing gaps. The diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Local construction and UTC/local/legacy setters now compose Number calendar values through the shared semantic component collection and MakeDay/MakeTime/MakeDate kernel. Source arguments no longer narrow into C calendar fields; the host time-zone adapter receives only normalized wall time, accepts the legitimate epoch second -1, and checks representability before host casts. Invocation prepares local wall time at its prescribed conversion checkpoint, including full-year/legacy invalid-date zero fallback. The local calendar switch/int/mktime normalization predecessors are deleted. `tests/property/date-local-calendar.property.test.ts` compares Node calendar/offset/clip partitions, independently reduces huge month integers to ordinary year-zero oracle dates, and verifies one deep retained calendar worklist through both native profiles and memory modes; `date_local_calendar` checks typed construction and setter arithmetic. Named time-zone political rules and repeated/skipped instant disambiguation still use the host adapter and need a canonical IANA transition representation. Ordinary Date construction lookup/coercion, parsing/text algorithms, remaining reciprocal Date clauses and signed host/report eligibility remain open. The pinned TimeClip and invalid-year selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-date78-pinned-clip.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date78-pinned-year.json`) pass with exact scenario/clause identities, unchanged source fingerprints, native completion and runner-attested C/executable manifests. The diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Boxed lexical cells now retain their decoded-root companion allocation through the source activation, including after its last store. The shared capture-cell allocator emits a volatile root-cell pointer; escaped environments retain the same cell handle. Canonical native application and construction retain encoded operands, materialized argument Lists, newTarget/prototype/receiver and proxy traps across allocation and callbacks; primitive conversion and Date toJSON retain their saved owners and methods. The previous standalone companion-root lifetime could reclaim an otherwise observable local Date: removing only the retention qualifier from generated C reproduces epoch-zero corruption with the runtime invocation roots present (`/tmp/tsc2c-date79-cell-baseline-run.log`). `tests/property/lexical-cell-lifetime.property.test.ts` verifies value-kind partitions after forced collection and one deep escaped-cell owner graph through CLI/native observation in both memory modes. Date invocation properties now also collect during construction coercion and between operations; typed `date_instance_invocation` passes both memory modes. Broader collectible callable-cache ownership and full language/host coverage remain open.

Date instance calls and member values now use ordinary retained-owner lookup, with the callable captured before actual argument effects. One intrinsic invocation captures the original DateValue before numeric coercion; UTC setters overlay present semantic components on that snapshot and use the shared Gregorian/Number kernel, without the C integer/timegm normalization predecessor. Non-year setters with a captured NaN return without overwriting mutations from coercion hooks; full-year setters use the prescribed zero fallback. Local/legacy setters share the snapshot and correct the invalid-date local-wall-time fallback, but their machine calendar/time-zone normalization remains open. Date's direct method lowerer and method/string purity predecessors are deleted. A single Date/Buffer owner admission collection also governs property reads and inferred binding layouts; one cached fixed point propagates actual-result facts through the expression/alias graph, and scalar reads and purity admission preserve those tagged values. CommonJS adapter probing visits each resolved binding owner once instead of replaying its alias chain through a duplicate name fallback. General callable return/field inference, other owner categories and wider native reflection remain open. Tests: `tests/property/date-instance-invocation.property.test.ts` (Node semantic partitions, independent pinned year-setter expectations where Node rereads after coercion, and one deep retained Date tree), `tests/property/ordinary-native-owner-result.property.test.ts` (independent Node Type/identity partitions and a representative deep source dependency), and `date_instance_invocation`, including typed aliases, closure retention and suspended calls. Full Date and signed host/report eligibility remain open. The pinned captured-NaN, year-snapshot and conversion-order selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-date77-pinned-snapshot.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date77-pinned-year.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date77-pinned-coercion.json`) pass natively with exact scenario/clause identities, unchanged source fingerprints and runner-attested C/executable manifests. The diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Separate CommonJS admission/binding gaps are reproduced on the pre-change emitter: `node_modules_commonjs_function_scope_require_alias` rejects the ambient require value without an ordinary owner, and `node_modules_commonjs_relative_require_binding_object_named` emits an undeclared local export value. Baseline evidence: `/tmp/tsc2c-date77-require-baseline.log` and `/tmp/tsc2c-date77-relative-baseline/main.c`. These remain open.

Date.now now returns an integral UTC millisecond time value by quantizing integer clock nanoseconds before epoch addition. Direct, retained, bound and reflective calls ignore their actual arguments; fresh Date construction shares the same clock. `tests/property/date-now.property.test.ts` uses independently sampled execution-time host bounds rather than a duplicate quantizer or expected timestamp and passes CLI/native observation in both memory modes. `date_now_time_value` covers typed consumers. Exact UTC and now clause partitions now bind reciprocal argument/coercion/calendar/clock/descriptor properties, declaration and implementation anchors, and the separately generated `date_static_argument_depth` retained owner-tree/actual-List stress. Source-hashed ambiguous generic constructability metadata preserves its original owner while adding each exact Date intrinsic. Date.parse, local/UTC instance methods, construction/time-zone semantics, remaining abstract-operation evidence and full signed host/report eligibility stay open. Complete pinned UTC and now selections (`/tmp/tsc2c-date76-pinned-utc.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date76-pinned-now.json`) pass natively with unchanged source fingerprints, complete exact intrinsic mappings and runner-owned artifacts; the diagnostic host remains ineligible.

UTC now consumes exact Gregorian integers through the shared nearest/even Number codec, mathematical month floor/modulo, a canonical month-start collection and explicit binary64 rounding points for MakeTime/MakeDate. MakeDay searches the ordered finite Number domain for an integral first-day witness using the monotone DayWithinYear predicate and adjacent integral-year TimeFromYear bounds; it introduces no machine year/month limit. TimeClip follows the completed operation, allowing prescribed overflow/cancellation and canonical positive zero. The UTC C integer/tm/timegm predecessor is deleted. `tests/property/date-utc-calendar.property.test.ts` compares an independent exact Gregorian model with Node in their shared range, separately verifies huge month cancellation and unrepresentable month witnesses against the pinned specification, and retains one deep calendar worklist through CLI/native observation in both memory modes; `date_utc_calendar` covers typed calls. Complete Date local construction/setters/parsing/time-zone semantics and the remaining exact reciprocal clauses stay open; UTC/now now have the reciprocal registrations above. The complete pinned UTC directory query (`/tmp/tsc2c-date75-pinned-utc.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints and runner-owned C/executable artifacts. The host remains ineligible; this is diagnostic evidence. The pre-fix native probe (`/tmp/tsc2c-date76-clock-baseline.log`) reproduced fractional Date.now results; the integral clock path above repairs that separate gap.

Native class static elements now use one source member collection and the retained constructor's ordinary descriptors. Methods and accessors are ordinary closures with actual this, arguments and formal initialization; fields are boxed data properties defined in static initialization order, and static blocks borrow the existing scope/capture cells. Represented inheritance links the constructor prototype to its base, preserving inherited receivers and own-field shadowing. Constructor reads retain mutations instead of reinstalling descriptors, and direct names and aliases use the same rooted property References. Ordinary and suspended GetValue/PutValue share referenced-name preparation, with compound reads before RHS evaluation and one cached ToPropertyKey result per the pinned edition. The native static globals, static C bodies/value adapters and direct static access/write predecessors are deleted. Unknown computed and private static keys fail closed at the common member-key boundary. Fresh repeated class evaluation, complete class environments/heritage, private/super semantics, dynamic computed names and broader class-expression elements remain open; whole-class coverage is not claimed. The pinned static-method default-length and field-initializer arrow-this queries (`/tmp/tsc2c-static73-pinned-length.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-static73-pinned-this.json`) pass natively with unchanged source fingerprints and runner-owned artifacts; the host remains ineligible and these reports do not establish whole-clause coverage.

Date statics now share ordinary method lookup, retained callee/receiver and the actual argument List. One runtime UTC parameter/default collection distinguishes omission from explicit undefined and converts present values in order even when an earlier value is non-finite. The typed direct now/parse/UTC path, emitter-local UTC defaults and static purity predecessor are deleted. Independent Node observations, generated presence/coercion/mutation partitions and one deep retained actual-List stress pass CLI and native observation in both memory modes at `tests/property/date-static-invocation.property.test.ts`; `date_static_invocation` and the awaited Date selector regression cover typed consumers.
