The pinned UTC coercion-order and return-value queries (`/tmp/tsc2c-date74-pinned-coercion.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-date74-pinned-return.json`) pass natively in strict and sloppy modes with unchanged source fingerprints and runner-owned C/executable artifacts; the diagnostic host remains ineligible.

Object.groupBy and Map.groupBy now share one rooted keyed-group collection over a live captured Iterator Record. Callback and property-key conversion failures close that record with incoming throw precedence; iterator-step failures propagate directly. Intrinsic result construction preserves null-prototype Object descriptors, Symbols, collection key equality and first-seen group order. Typed Map and Set constructors use the same ordinary constructor/iterator protocol, with checked in-place native storage projection; the eager grouping and copied constructor loops are deleted. Source-native aggregates retain one ordinary object owner and actual-type member initializer, while native entries retain their actual pair Array instead of duplicated fields. Nullish cells remain tagged, and BigInt/callable keys retain language equality. Independent Node comparisons, generated semantic partitions and one deep retained grouping tree at `tests/property/group-by.property.test.ts` exercise CLI and native observation in both memory modes; `group_by_iterator_records` covers typed consumers and alias mutation. Remaining Realm intrinsics (including Array.fromAsync), Script/eval environments, asynchronous Module evaluation, class/private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open. The pinned property-key and callback-throw queries (`/tmp/tsc2c-group72-pinned-property-key.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-group72-pinned-callback.json`) pass natively in strict and sloppy modes with unchanged source fingerprints and runner-attested C/executables; they remain diagnostic evidence and do not establish whole-clause coverage. The diagnostic host remains ineligible and full ECMAScript coverage is not established.

The Realm global object and compiler admission now project one shared binding descriptor collection. Free JavaScript names and admitted intrinsic bindings use the retained Environment Record get/set/delete/typeof path in CLI and native observation; mutable globalThis reads no longer become an unresolved C identifier or a hardwired Realm object. Ordinary calls, construction and member reads observe replacement values and descriptor effects, while source declarations shadow globals and host APIs keep their adapters. Object static callables reuse existing runtime operations, with actual-List assignment and one integrity-operation collection. Independent Node comparisons, generated descriptor/Reference partitions and one deep retained-owner tree pass Script and Module native E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/global-reference.property.test.ts`; `global_reference_cells` covers typed retained and suspended reads. Complete Realm intrinsic installation (including Array.fromAsync callables), general Script/eval environments outside the observation profile, asynchronous Module evaluation, remaining grammar/class/private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open. The diagnostic host remains ineligible; full ECMAScript coverage is not established. The pinned global-object query (`/tmp/tsc2c-global71-pinned-object.json`) passes natively in strict and sloppy modes with unchanged source fingerprints and the shared binding table included in runner-attested artifacts.

Resource declarations now pass shared source-tree static semantics before emission. The Await context follows parse goals and independent Function/static-block boundaries; declaration owners, loop-head grammar, trivia and BoundNames constraints use the same worklist and Set. Bare Script and switch-clause resources, exported declaration syntax and invalid async contexts fail with stable diagnostics; block-owned switch resources remain legal. Compiler validation, emission and eval discovery share raw-token Use Strict recognition, replacing cooked-string prologue scans. Independent Node grammar comparisons, a primary-spec assertion for escaped literal terminals, compiler early-error checks and one deep legal scope tree pass CLI and native observation E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/resource-syntax.property.test.ts`; `resource_context_syntax_diagnostic` and `resource_completion_protocol` cover typed consumers. The CLI globalThis admission gap is repaired through the shared Realm Reference path described above. Remaining lexical-scope early errors, async Module resource evaluation, stack APIs, class/private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open. The pinned nullish-resource query (`/tmp/tsc2c-resource70-pinned-nullish.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints. The diagnostic host is ineligible and resource clauses remain unmapped.

Resource declaration cells now share ordinary BindingInitialization and retained Module value/root/TDZ storage. Each scope owns one captured DisposeCapability collection; the shared completion worklist drains it in reverse acquisition order across ordinary exits, suspension and generator requests. Acquisition validates/captures methods before binding publication, failures retain the original raw value through intrinsic SuppressedError chains, and async cleanup suspends only for semantic Await operands, including nullish resources and sync fallback. Name-list disposal, lazy method rereads and the aggregate async disposal Promise are deleted. Independent Node comparisons, generated acquisition/completion partitions and representative deep scope trees pass CLI and native observation E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/resource-completion.property.test.ts` and `tests/property/resumable-resource.property.test.ts`; `resource_completion_protocol` checks the typed surface. The pinned sync-fallback disposal query (`/tmp/tsc2c-resource69-pinned-sync-fallback.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints. Async Module evaluation/top-level await using, complete resource early-error contexts and disposable-stack APIs, class/private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open. The resource feature tag still has no pinned clause mapping and the diagnostic host remains ineligible; this work does not establish full ECMAScript coverage.

JSON default imports and their local, named and star re-exports now resolve through the AOT Module Record graph with one visited (module, export name) set. Checker SourceFile aliases no longer conflate a synthetic default value with a namespace Reference. Ordinary reads, namespace getters and retained Module environments borrow the same rooted default cell, while namespace identity and star-resolution equality use the target Module Record. The direct-name JSON scan and single-step namespace re-export lookup are deleted. Independent comparisons with the Node JSON loader, generated root/import/write partitions, graph diamonds and cycles, and one deep module graph with retained owners pass CLI and native observation E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/json-module-environment.property.test.ts`. Resource declaration cells, complete class/private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open; the diagnostic host remains ineligible. The pinned JSON object query (`/tmp/tsc2c-json68-pinned-object.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints; it remains diagnostic evidence and does not promote whole-clause coverage.

Module variable cells now derive every BoundName from the existing binding tree, including nested patterns and vars declared in module control statements. One BindingInitialization traversal initializes ordinary declarations and iteration leaves into those value/root/TDZ cells, preserving ordered defaults, computed/rest properties, iterator close precedence and empty-pattern effects. Export and namespace reads resolve BindingElement declarations to the same live cells; source closures retain Module records instead of copying module bindings, while explicit dispatch snapshots keep their cloning semantics. The separate flat var/lexical initializers and single-declaration symbol tables are deleted. Independent Node comparisons, generated declaration/pattern partitions and one deep nested binding-tree stress pass native E2E through the CLI and observation profile in both memory modes at `tests/property/module-binding-tree.property.test.ts`. Resource declaration cell admission, complete class/private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open; the diagnostic host remains ineligible. The pinned Module var binding query (`/tmp/tsc2c-module67-pinned-var.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints; it remains diagnostic evidence and does not promote whole-clause coverage.

Source Modules in the native observation profile now retain Module Environment Records over the ordinary value/root/TDZ binding collection. Vars, lexicals, instantiated function values and immutable live import aliases share their source cells; function owners are captured during declaration instantiation and namespace reads use the same mutable bindings. The Environment Record context boundary supplies undefined module this and rejects new.target before eval declarations are published. Source discovery preserves explicit Script/Module parse goals independently of checker isolation markers; generated evaluation records keep the Script goal. The source table distinguishes executable roots from attested resources, and compiler dependency discovery loads static and dynamic import targets without admitting unrelated siblings. Independent Node comparisons, generated parse-goal and import-write partitions and one deep retained module-owner tree pass native E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/module-eval-context.property.test.ts`. Resource declaration cell admission, complete native-class environments, private/super/with/Annex B contexts, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open; unrepresented Module cells fail closed and the diagnostic host remains ineligible. The pinned Module eval Script-goal and live-import queries (`/tmp/tsc2c-module66-pinned-script-goal-final.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-module66-pinned-live-import-final.json`) pass natively with unchanged source fingerprints; they remain diagnostic evidence and do not promote whole-clause coverage.

Ordinary and resumed calls now apply one saved callee/receiver/actual-List operation that preserves direct eval Reference mode. Shared caller-scope projection runs after argument evaluation, retaining lexical cells and function context across await/yield, replacement of eval, optional indirect calls and abrupt arguments. Resumable states transfer implicit eval closure captures through the existing field-handle collection; source discovery shares mutable global property cells and compiler admission chooses the direct or indirect registry from that same invocation record. The separate direct-eval argument assembly path is deleted. Independent Node comparisons, generated Reference-mode/strictness partitions and one deep retained suspended-owner tree pass native E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/suspended-eval-context.property.test.ts`. Module Environment Record integration, private/super/with/Annex B contexts, native-module callable owners, non-finite parsing and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open; the diagnostic host remains ineligible. The pinned optional indirect-eval query (`/tmp/tsc2c-call65-pinned-optional-final.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints; it remains diagnostic evidence and does not promote a whole-clause claim.

Finite eval discovery now consumes the same source-value, binding/property location and actual-List graph as dynamic Function discovery. Eval identities, formals/returns, getter and bound targets, spread/apply Lists and registered evaluation completions feed one exact-source fixed point. Eval preserves non-String identity, while evalScript converts proven primitive inputs; compiler admission uses the same graph and parenthesized eval preserves direct References. The name-based alias and literal-only discovery predecessors are deleted. Independent Node comparisons, generated call-mode/strictness partitions and one deep source-cell dependency chain pass native E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/eval-source-flow.property.test.ts`. Context-sensitive source specialization, general object ToPrimitive, complete mutable/iterator Lists, caller-dependent evaluation completion flow and non-finite runtime parsing remain open; unknown sources fail closed and the diagnostic host remains ineligible. The pinned direct non-String query (`/tmp/tsc2c-flow64-pinned-nonstring.json`) passes natively with unchanged source fingerprints; it is diagnostic evidence and does not promote a whole-clause claim.

Finite direct eval now projects source scopes through the same retained Environment Record tree and heap value/root/TDZ cells used by ordinary closures and resumable states. Caller reads and writes share cells; function variable records preserve sloppy-eval insertions and deletion, including closures created before eval and vars that shadow outer bindings. Parameter expressions retain separate parameter/body records and copied body var cells. Function context retains actual this, mapped or unmapped arguments and new.target, with early context rejection before eval declaration publication; sloppy receiver boxing occurs once. One source worklist discovers nested eval and per-call strictness, replacing the switch/global-var eligibility predecessors. Independent Node execution, generated declaration/strictness partitions and one deep retained-caller tree pass native E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/direct-eval-cell.property.test.ts`. Class/private/super/with environments, complete Annex B and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open; the diagnostic host remains ineligible. Diagnostic caller-variable, retained template-cache and new.target context queries (`/tmp/tsc2c-env63-pinned-caller-final.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-env63-pinned-template-final.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-env63-pinned-context-final.json`) pass natively with unchanged source fingerprints; they do not promote whole-clause coverage.

Finite eval declarations now use one retained declarative Environment Record tree and binding collection. Sloppy eval keeps lexical names local and inserts vars/functions into the enclosing variable environment; strict eval keeps both local. Shared lookup preserves TDZ, immutable writes, deletion and global fallback. Source callable identity, deferred owners, construction, resumable callbacks and abrupt transfer retain or restore that tree with Realm and parse-unit context. Indirect eval, evalScript and Function construction begin at the global boundary. Independent engine declaration/context partitions and one deep retained-parent input pass source-to-native E2E in both memory modes at `tests/property/eval-environment.property.test.ts`. Caller lexical-cell, variable insertion and this/arguments/new.target context now use the shared projection above; complete class ownership, Annex B and super/private eval context remain open; the native host is still ineligible and no whole-clause coverage is promoted. Diagnostic direct lexical-isolation and indirect TDZ reports (`/tmp/tsc2c-env62-pinned-direct.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-env62-pinned-indirect.json`) pass with unchanged source fingerprints.

Tagged templates now use one source-owned UTF-16 segment collection and a Realm TemplateMap keyed by the source Parse Node and retained runtime parse unit. Cached cooked/raw arrays preserve invalid-escape undefined values, normalized raw line terminators, intrinsic Array prototypes and frozen index/length/raw descriptors. Ordinary and resumable tags share actual callable/receiver/argument Lists; mutable String.raw uses ordinary lookup. The shared property-write path preserves strict rejected assignments and updates, and property deletion preserves strict failure. Independent engine partitions, generated lexical/value cases and one deep retained-parse-owner input pass under forced collection in both memory modes; typed E2E is at `tests/e2e/cases/tagged_template_sites`. Caller lexical capture now uses the shared cell projection; private/super References, broader class parse ownership and exact reciprocal GetTemplateObject clause evidence remain open. Diagnostic pinned template-object and invalid-escape reports (`/tmp/tsc2c-template61-pinned-object-final.json`, `/tmp/tsc2c-template61-pinned-escapes-final.json`) pass with unchanged source fingerprints; the prior broader cache discovery admission gap is repaired by the shared caller-cell projection. The native host remains ineligible and these reports do not promote whole-clause coverage.

Finite constructor discovery now follows source values through bindings, object fields/getters, source formals/returns, registered eval completions, bound targets and argument-List sequences whose elements use shared property locations, including later writes. One monotone source graph closes these dependencies without inferring receiver/member types; cyclic spread/text dependencies retain the finite-manifest boundary. Primitive addition and template conversion preserve source argument text. JavaScript binding emission retains initializer effects, derives callable receiver/parameter/result storage from source syntax, and avoids unused structural inference when boxed storage is already selected. Boxed property assignments precede native accessor probes; boxed rest arguments use the shared actual-List builder. Independent execution records actual constructor source keys, and a retained source-owner stress input plus typed E2E exercise the native registry in both memory modes. Broader class/prototype/global-member and yielded-result flows, complete mutable Array/List and ToPrimitive derivation, and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open; unknown runtime sources retain the explicit manifest/unsafe-eval gate.

Runtime parse ownership is independent of AOT code reuse. Each finite Function construction or native-host eval creates a fresh parse-unit token; canonical callable keys/owners retain it together with creation Realm, including deferred boxing. Script function declarations instantiate fresh closure owners on each evaluation. Calls, construction, resumable callbacks and exception transfer restore the retained context. Independent ParseText/closure/method/accessor/Realm/suspension/abrupt partitions and one deep retained-owner tree pass in both memory modes, with typed E2E at `tests/e2e/cases/dynamic_source_owners`. Tagged-template site descriptors, Realm TemplateMap construction and shared ordinary/resumable invocation are implemented through the canonical segment and call worklists; broader class/eval environments and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open.

Current resumable-expression status: one explicit operation worklist retains saved References, actual callee/receiver/argument Lists and partial literal owners through suspension. Async conditions, declaration/assignment initializers, nested awaits and return/throw expressions consume that graph; direct-await, logical/conditional and stable-prefix expression planners are deleted. Independent effect-order/reference/key/abrupt/GC partitions and the same representative deep expression input exercise the model and native consumer, with typed Node E2E calibration. The pinned edition caches GetValue/PutValue referenced names; Node still reconverts compound-assignment keys, and Bun currently skips arguments at a parenthesized optional-callee boundary, so those partitions use pinned-algorithm assertions. Maximal synchronous subtrees retain ordinary native call adapters; native-module callee values for suspending argument Lists remain an admission gap. The pinned Await syntax report `/tmp/tsc2c-resumable52-pinned-syntax-final.json` is diagnostic evidence with unchanged source and an ineligible host, not whole-clause evidence. Ordinary and async generator consumers now project this graph through shared activation/completion records, lifecycle/FIFO request worklists and rooted delegation phases. Private/super references, resumable destructuring assignments, broader class operations and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open.

Current Function-constructor status: finite joined parameter/body pairs close transitively through one AOT source worklist and fresh ordinary function factories. Shared formal/body/activation lowering preserves actual receiver/argument values, retained cells and creation Realm. Runtime conversion, prototype lookup, anonymous SourceText and actual SyntaxError partitions pass independent properties, a deep retained-function graph and typed E2E checks. Callable owners now retain available JavaScript definition spans; toString uses internal source/InitialName without user-property access, and ordinary string coercion shares that path. Typed-source erasure, broader class admission and embedded-bridge source availability remain open alongside complete JS source early errors (including broader declaration/private-name rules), complete value-flow/text/actual-List derivation, full GlobalEnv integration outside Script mode and unsafe-bridge error/Realm interoperability. Unknown runtime sources still require an explicit manifest or the existing unsafe-eval gate.

Ordinary descriptor maps now share runtime reflection and actual call Lists. Numeric key ordering, dynamic length values, Array element codecs, live native field data descriptors and completed Proxy descriptor results have independent properties and typed E2E coverage. The diagnostic pinned query still cannot prove the exact clause until harness nested-function admission and mutable global Object bindings are repaired. Native platform owners (including Error/Stats/Dirent metadata), complete class identity/field admission, Function prototype creation chronology and Proxy ownKeys target invariants also remain open; replacing their legacy typed projections must repair their shared owner records. Full ECMAScript2026 and arbitrary node_modules coverage remains incomplete.

The shortest-number and requested decimal formatters, ordinary locale invocation and shared for declaration environments pass independent semantic properties and typed end-to-end checks. Typed Number text/value methods use ordinary lookup, actual argument Lists and shared ToNumber option coercion. Their exact intrinsic rows and the Number locale choice now bind reciprocal evidence and a generated argument-List stress; the remaining Number/BigInt/Math/Date clauses, other native locale projections, native call admission/defaults, physical return-result inference and suspended reference/lookup graphs remain open. Local evidence does not establish the full signed claim. Complete function-scoped pattern instantiation, Script loop completion values, dynamic evaluation environments, head disposal records and lazy-generator suspension graphs also remain open. Iteration lexical head/body conflicts, duplicate names and required initializers now use the shared early-error worklist; full loop grammar/Annex B validation remains open. Typed array literals contextually inferred as tuple representations can still reject a valid partial destructuring source; ordinary boxed binding-pattern sources are verified.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **CRITICAL — prove cardinality independently.** Do not enumerate fixture families, successive await/yield/argument/branch counts, or narrate their pass counts as evidence of generality. Prove arbitrary cardinality from the canonical collection/tree/graph/worklist representation and lowering algorithm; verify semantic partitions with compact generated/property tests plus one representative high-depth stress case. A finite fixture inventory may detect regressions, but it must never define the implementation plan, completion units, or proof of coverage.

# TODO — remaining work

Current reflection status: Object descriptor/key/enumeration and prototype membership/accessor calls and Reflect use ordinary lookup and rooted actual Lists. EnumerableOwnProperties shares one key snapshot with ordered descriptor/Get operations across represented owners. Node properties and native E2E exercise mutation, coercion order, abrupt identity, Symbols, UTF-16 and retained values. Finish native platform owner metadata, remaining Object statics/CJS static-proof bypasses, complete Function source early errors/source descriptors and exact constructor-kind clause evidence, complete Proxy invariants, UTF-8 output for unpaired surrogates and exact reciprocal evidence. Retained body binding cells supersede the former literal-stack storage assumptions; source/output regressions remain, while any renewed escape/allocation optimization must preserve the canonical binding representation.

Current identifier storage status: binding and field spellings share a reversible UTF-16 component encoder; module and namespace ownership use framed component Lists rather than flattened names. Independent reversibility/depth, rooted field and Node E2E checks cover that path. Native class/interface owners now share a source-declaration identity and one AST registry across type mapping, layouts, constructors, fields, adapters and native inheritance branding; public metadata retains its spelling. Independent module/namespace/layout/brand and retained-owner properties verify the represented path. Audit remaining compiler-generated naming domains before claiming complete identifier hygiene or exact Identifier clause evidence. Native member physical names now share the framed scope/kind/key plan across definitions, adapters, calls and specializations, so static/instance and accessor/String/Symbol domains remain distinct. Native class for-of uses ordinary iterator records and lexical binding cells; generic method values reuse erased specialization bindings. Merged source interfaces fail closed until declaration merging has a canonical owner plan. Complete method activation/default/rest plans, duplicate member replacement and ordinary class object identity remain open.

Scope of the full project (from the approved plan): **full ECMAScript 2026 + transpile arbitrary `node_modules`**.

Items are grouped by how soon they unblock the most user value. Within each group the ordering reflects dependency order and implementation leverage. Completed work belongs in [`done.md`](done.md), not in this TODO file.

---

## ECMAScript 2026 compliance workflow

The canonical work inputs are [`../compliance/ecmascript-2026/baseline.json`](../compliance/ecmascript-2026/baseline.json), [`../compliance/ecmascript-2026/feature-map.json`](../compliance/ecmascript-2026/feature-map.json), [`../compliance/ecmascript-2026/matrix.json`](../compliance/ecmascript-2026/matrix.json), and the reciprocal [`../compliance/ecmascript-2026/evidence-registry.json`](../compliance/ecmascript-2026/evidence-registry.json). The generated human checklist is [`ecmascript-2026-coverage.md`](ecmascript-2026-coverage.md). It is a work view, never an authored support verdict. Only `bun run compliance:claim` may emit claim wording, and the checked-in profile deliberately blocks that claim today.

Current Buffer projection status: native byte records retain canonical ordinary owners and direct backing-allocation roots. Realm method descriptors, live integer-indexed object hooks and the shared Number byte plan now serve typed and boxed calls, method values, aliases, mutation, reflection and iterator consumers. Buffer.from uses one indexed byte-source traversal; slices share bytes and perform ordinary subarray lookup. Independent polynomial/DataView properties, dual-memory forced collection and one deep owner chain/wide argument List verify this partition; Node agrees with the new typed E2E case. Inline Array HOF parameter storage now agrees with body emission, and own descriptor collection uses the ordinary key/GetOwnProperty worklist. Full Uint8Array/TypedArray constructors and shared backing ArrayBuffer/detachment, generic borrowed-method receivers, complete encoding/search/text/fill/copy/compare overload and conversion-order semantics, remaining Buffer static/global lookup paths, proxy invariants on integer-indexed targets and exact reciprocal platform evidence remain open. This projection does not establish complete Buffer, TypedArray, Node or arbitrary-package coverage.

Current lexical-block status: ordinary Blocks and CaseBlocks share a direct declaration list and scope-entry value/TDZ/root cells; supported functions are created freshly before statements, including declarations after an abrupt statement. The async CFG carries the same list and function closures across suspension. Strict/sloppy properties, one retained-environment stress and typed consumers verify this partition in both memory modes. Ordinary function-body instantiation and async activation now have independent implementation properties and E2E evidence; exact FunctionDeclarationInstantiation registration, local Annex B companion bindings, non-empty classes, async generators, lazy-generator block environments and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open. The wrapped CommonJS callable fixture now passes through retained host records; complete Node format detection/interoperability remains open.

Current Module-host status: the complete ModuleBody early-error rule list now uses shared canonical name, statement/control-tree, `Contains`, lexical-private-environment, and Unicode-code-unit worklists. The formerly blocked positive nested-class private-name case executes through the dynamic class-expression method/capture plan, the exact pinned clause query is represented by reciprocal matrix evidence, and the local diagnostic slice is clean. Keep `module-goal-and-fixtures` disabled until asynchronous cyclic evaluation/top-level await and the remaining loading/evaluation partitions are complete and an exhaustive signed report is clean; clause evidence is not host eligibility.

Current ArrowFunction status: ordinary JavaScript arrows now share one FormalParameters binding tree and recursive BindingInitialization path, including defaults/rest/destructuring/TDZ/capture. One ordered lexical-environment descriptor collection snapshots `this`, `arguments`, and `new.target` through escaped, nested, call/apply/bind, mapped/unmapped, alternate-constructor, and parameter-initializer paths; the representative generated JavaScript stress traverses the same environment tree at depth. The shared frontend/native-host checker covers the complete Early Errors and `ConciseBodyContainsUseStrict` partitions. Their reciprocal property, feature/path/legacy mappings, and exact syntax matrix rows are present, and the pinned syntax plus lexical-arguments/new-target probes are locally clean in diagnostic mode. Do not register the parent, `EvaluateConciseBody`, instantiation, or evaluation clauses until lexical `super`, name/length/source-text/private-environment behavior, eval/body environment interactions, and every exact pinned clause query are complete. Diagnostic success is not host or signed-report eligibility.

Current Array ABI identity status: boxed/native boundaries borrow matching codecs from one owner and use an exact round-trip raw-slot worklist for admissible boxed storage; newly created literals may choose their initial representation before exposure. Identity, aliases, metadata, holes, getter order and a deep owner graph pass independent dual-memory checks. Incompatible existing native views and values that cannot preserve SameValue fail with a stable diagnostic; repair shared views and class/function/entry boxing before admitting those representations. The old conversion-copy paths are removed. Ordinary generators now use a separate ordinary object owner and boxed Iterator ABI; remaining native Array consumers and full GeneratorFunction/constructor intrinsic metadata still need shared object-model work. Boxed Symbol.iterator calls now preserve actual receivers, argument Lists and lookup order; the remaining native Array/String/URLSearchParams/class Symbol-call shortcuts still need ordinary protocol migration. Fresh function/arrow creation now bypasses the unnecessary contextual nullable-type query at the shared boxing boundary; the Promise resolution property is clean again in both memory modes. The Promise combinator timeout is now repaired by one keyed callable registry; the existing queued-element stress completes with correct observations in both memory modes. The old global list and its duplicated callable/accessor/listener scans are deleted. The preceding compiler failure and timeout were reproduced on unchanged 8d881600a, separately from the Array ABI change. Full user-function source-text retention in Function.prototype.toString and native listener wrappers remains open. Native function records now retain owner-held identities across boxing/signature adaptation, but callable identities still have globally retained cache lifetimes; ownership for remaining callable sources and cache lifetime work remain necessary for collectible function graphs and identity-preserving boxed/native function adaptation.

Current native aggregate ownership status: boxed fields retain decoded roots in their owner through the shared field store, including static cells, construction, literals/spreads, reflection, compound/logical writes and updates. The canonical ancestor worklist now includes the immediate base; native interface boxing exposes the same field adapters, and reads preserve declared physical storage after flow narrowing. Independent dual-memory properties and one deep owner graph verify this partition. Internal-field collisions, inherited physical type changes and legacy suspended boxed-field lvalues fail closed; repair their canonical layout/reference representation before removing those diagnostics. Ordinary class field descriptors/wrapper identity, structural class/interface layout conversion, constructor/derived-field initialization order and full class semantics remain open. TypeScript's inferred number storage for a boxed update/arithmetic result can still coerce a runtime BigInt; explicit any result storage is covered, but general physical expression-result inference remains to be repaired.

Current Number formatting status: exact binary64 midpoint intervals and radix normalization select minimal significant digits with nearest/even candidates. Requested decimal precision shares exact coefficient rounding and dynamic layout, choosing larger positive halfway values and preserving fixed/significant widths and carries. Number coercion, JSON, radix and decimal formatters share the digit representation; capped snprintf/manual rounding predecessors and observable integer shortcuts are deleted. Primitive wrappers retain boxed identity and [[PrimitiveData]]. Ordinary Realm-owned methods and typed calls preserve mutable lookup, actual argument Lists, receiver validation, number-hint option coercion and each method's non-finite/range ordering. Independent rational interval/minimality and rounding-cell properties, typed Node observations and representative output/coercion stresses pass in both memory modes. Numeric locale text follows the documented non-402 fallback through ordinary methods. Exact intrinsic rows now bind reciprocal evidence, one generated actual argument-List stress and the Number locale implementation choice. Number statics now use ordinary Realm-owned predicate/constant descriptors and canonical global parser aliases, and shared primitive constructor prototype descriptors are non-writable. Independent binary64 semantic partitions and the shared actual-List stress are locally clean; exact static/parent clause registration, remaining Number constructor/global binding paths, other Number/BigInt/Math/Date and full signed coverage remain open.

Current locale status: Object, Array and numeric locale methods share rooted ordinary invocation, actual receiver/results and exact errors. Array length conversion precedes one live indexed worklist; non-nullish elements receive empty argument Lists and returned values use string-hint conversion. The non-402 runtime selects intrinsic Number/BigInt decimal text, comma separation and ignored reserved options. Native primitive/Array calls and method values respect lookup and mutation, and shared ordinary-method purity admission retains observable calls. Independent dual-memory properties and a wide element traversal are clean. Symbol's ordinary description accessor now initializes with the prototype rather than depending on constructor lookup. Targeted Number/BigInt/Object pinned diagnostics are clean; Array's resizable-buffer scenarios remain blocked by unknown dynamic Function source in the harness, so its complete clause stays open. The Number locale clause and implementation choice now bind reciprocal evidence. Other native Date, typed-array, class and Node-object locale projections, the remaining locale choice records and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open. Buffer's owner/byte-method/locale-alias partition is now represented and verified above; the prior lexical-cell byte-method and addition failures were reproduced on unchanged 04902927b. Dirent reflection still cannot box its native representation.

Current addition status: ordinary source trees lower through one postorder worklist into flat staged values, with one shared operand dispatcher for native primitives and ordinary default-hint addition. Runtime conversion results retain decoded roots across the other operand conversion. Native BigInt operand snapshot failure was reproduced on unchanged 8d881600a. Try/catch/finally Blocks now enter canonical lexical scopes, including escaped catch-body bindings. Independent semantic properties and one deep tree pass in both memory modes. Wider binary operator staging, complete suspension graphs, physical result inference and reciprocal exact clause evidence remain open.

Current string accumulation status: ordinary local/module/capture storage replaces the separate mutable buffer binding representation. Native compound addition stages the reference and old value before the RHS; shared value addition preserves default conversion hints and source grouping. The prior undeclared-buffer compilation defect was reproduced on unchanged 8d881600a. Independent semantic properties and a long accumulation stress pass in both memory modes. Repeated immutable append performance still needs a semantics-preserving runtime representation; other expression operators and suspension graphs, native compound references outside this partition and exact reciprocal clause evidence remain open.

Current ordinary logical-value status: &&/||/?? share a postorder expression worklist and one actual-operand selector with non-suspending generator-resume expressions. Nullable unions retain Null/Undefined in boxed declaration, call, field and return storage; shared boxing preserves legacy native nullable producers. Independent properties, one deep logical tree and typed/neighboring checks verify this partition in both memory modes. Register exact reciprocal expression clauses only after their complete pinned partitions pass. Array boundaries now preserve identity for admissible codecs and fail closed on incompatible views. Native function conversion now preserves actual identity and creation metadata through an owner-held value/root; the canonical parameter List carries its rest position through returns, getters and differing native signatures. Callable Array codec witnesses retain SameValue. General incompatible Array views, class/entry boxing, native call admission/default activation, complete source metadata and callable cache lifetimes remain open. C identifier hygiene also remains incomplete: a valid top-level identifier such as nan can collide with an included libc declaration.

Current async logical-value status: value-producing `&&`, `||`, and `??` now use one recursive CFG result-slot graph for arbitrary left subtrees and evaluate each left owner once; the former stable-identifier/property admission restriction is removed. The independent graph invariant, dual-memory semantic partition, and separate generated high-depth tree are architectural evidence only. Do not infer whole AsyncFunction or expression-clause coverage from the pending-return fixture: exact terminal clause partitions still need reciprocal matrix/Test262/implementation registration, and all unrelated async, generator, host, and signed-report obligations remain open.

Retained DCE regression: the previous Set/Object.values typed boundary is repaired. The fresh standalone project checker now accepts generated_c_dce_const after its Iterator array reads and string-or-symbol comparator inputs are corrected. Final native GC/no-GC checks both terminate at EventTarget.addEventListener.capture requiring a Boolean literal, including an ordinary omitted/undefined option. Repair the shared actual-value dictionary path and preserve ordinary mutable callee effects when reviewing older purity expectations.

Current Array iterator status: Array keys/values/entries now create ordinary Realm-owned iterator objects over one source/index/kind record. Each next reads the live LengthOfArrayLike and indexed property, retains completion, and preserves getter order, reentrancy, holes, inherited values and numeric entry keys. Array.from uses actual iterator/next methods for dynamic and typed Array sources, including arguments; the array/string fast consumers are deleted. Iterator-producing receivers escape stack allocation, and entry codecs include both key and value types. Ordinary generators now use the shared resumable graph and an ordinary object/protocol owner; the legacy Array generator brand, queues and fixed templates are deleted. TypedArray buffer witness/detachment, remaining typed String factory paths, full GeneratorFunction/constructor intrinsics, async generators, the Iterator constructor/remaining helpers and exact clause evidence remain open.

Current matchAll status: String/RegExp matchAll now use ordinary symbol lookup, ordered shared SpeciesConstructor, native/custom RegExpExec and a Realm-owned lazy RegExp String Iterator state record. Each next yields complete exec metadata, advances empty matches by the snapshotted Unicode mode and retains completion/abrupt retry semantics; the original RegExp lastIndex remains separate. Shared iterator results and materialization support Array.from and typed/dynamic literal spread through existing element codecs, with decoded roots during list growth. The eager native/emitter matchAll paths and its unsafe purity admission are deleted. Independent properties, typed tests, the existing deep RegExp pattern stress and the pinned match-string coercion-error diagnostic pass. Split, remaining native collection and resumable/spread consumers, remaining grammar and exact clause evidence stay open.

Current synchronous for-of status: ordinary dynamic/Array/String/Map/Set sources now use one iterator record and lexical owner, stepping before each binding/body instead of materializing the source. C control edges unwind owners in reverse lexical order; binding/body throws close with original-error precedence, while abrupt next/done/value evaluation completes its record without closing. Head TDZ and fresh per-iteration value/TDZ/root environments feed the shared BindingName tree, including captured nested patterns. Global reference setters now root fresh values before key allocation, repairing a frequent-collection prototype-chain failure exposed by the descriptor regression. Independent semantic properties, one deep runtime scope stress, typed consumers, neighboring properties and pinned IteratorClose/head-environment diagnostics verify this partition. Synchronous finalizers now join protected regions and iterator owners in one completion worklist; disposal scopes remain an explicit diagnostic boundary. Function var-pattern environments, assignment-pattern/member references, ordinary Buffer/URLSearchParams/custom-class object protocols, async/generator continuation integration and exact clause evidence remain open; legacy native collection adapters are not ordinary protocol coverage.

Current synchronous completion status: return, break and continue leave the same suffix of one active protected-region/iterator scope collection. Exited handlers are removed before callbacks; finally and IteratorClose follow lexical order and abrupt finalizers override pending completions. Symbol-indexed reference projections preserve outer bindings under inner shadowing, and pending values retain decoded roots. The duplicated try emitters are deleted. Script try/catch/finally completion values now apply UpdateEmpty with undefined; the old empty-catch property expectation was corrected against the pinned algorithm and an independent host observation. Independent control/Script properties, one deep runtime scope input, typed async adoption and pinned finalizer diagnostics verify this partition. Canonical disposal records, hoisted/TDZ bindings without an available environment, general async/generator continuation integration and exact clause evidence remain open.

Current collection protocol status: Map/Set share stable insertion links over dense native storage and one live cursor algorithm. Deletion, clearing, updating and reinsertion preserve retained iterator positions; completed cursors remain complete. Realm-owned constructor/prototype descriptors, cached instance identity, aliases, next branding and forEach actual calls use shared element codecs and rooted values. JavaScript constructors observe actual adders and iterators, closing consumer failures with original-error precedence. Typed for-of, factories and forEach use ordinary lookup; fixed loop/callback adapters are deleted. Shared synchronous records now live in tsc_iterator.c, with direct-record forEach/reduce consumers. Static module projection requires explicit iterator materialization. Independent semantic properties, one live-churn stress, typed consumers and pinned iterator/constructor diagnostics verify these paths in both memory modes. Typed writable-domain/class identity and constructor intake, Set composition, remaining Iterator helpers/constructor, resumable consumers and reciprocal exact clause evidence remain open.

Current Promise intake status: all/race/any/allSettled now interleave one ordinary iterator record with the actual constructor resolve property and returned then method. Generic capabilities, one ordered result/error List, a remaining sentinel and per-element gates preserve callback and closing semantics; allSettled shares its gate across both outcomes. The separate eager runtime/emitter loops, materializers and combinator purity shortcuts are deleted. Newly created intrinsic AggregateErrors expose non-enumerable errors without an invented message. Awaited results use the shared Promise codec and Array conversion reads actual element codecs, preserving nested typed payloads. Typed class boxing retains well-known Symbol method keys in the existing inherited method collection. Independent dual-memory properties, one queued-element stress, typed/neighboring checks and targeted pinned diagnostics verify these paths. Promise.try's argument diagnostic remains clean. Exact reciprocal Promise/Array/Error/class evidence, complete native class object identity/computed keys, function-body declaration instantiation/local Annex B companions, Array.fromAsync and broader resumable/host obligations remain open.

Current throw payload status: throw/rethrow share one value/decoded-root/diagnostic completion record and transfer through active frames without user coercion. Ordinary catches and runtime/emitter promise rejection bridges preserve the original value; host diagnostics inspect intrinsic storage separately. Async function emitters share a synchronous rejection boundary, and settled promises root values before allocation. Independent payload/diagnostic properties, one deep rethrow input, typed callback/thenable/async identity and pinned throw-Array diagnostics verify this partition. The separate ordinary Promise implementation now unblocks the pinned reaction diagnostic. Remaining continuation/dispatch string state, async parameter environments, wider runtime rooting and exact clause evidence remain open.

Current construction-root status: the frequent-collection replacement crash reproduced on preceding commit 42c5b7458 is repaired. Unfinished ordinary object literals retain a volatile decoded target through their complete property worklist, including CommonJS and resumable literal construction; accessor installation retains targets/callbacks before key conversion and environment allocation. The descriptor property forces collection during computed-key construction, and descriptor/replacement properties run the GC build with GC_FREE_SPACE_DIVISOR=100 alongside the allocation-only build. The retained replay now completes normally. This closes the demonstrated lifetime defect only; it is not proof of all runtime rooting or host eligibility.

Current descriptor status: fixed descriptor-literal parsing and typed-array definition emitters are deleted. Ordinary object evaluation now feeds one ordered runtime ToPropertyDescriptor record for Object/Reflect definitions, descriptor maps, and Object.create; methods, accessors, spreads, computed fields, unknown-field side effects, inherited fields, Symbols, aliases, complete map preparation, and real TypeErrors are covered by independent dual-memory properties and typed E2E checks. Typed array indexed reads and joins observe metadata through element codecs; deep inherited prototypes and descriptor callback/value records retain decoded GC roots. The pinned RegExp flags-tostring-error diagnostic now passes. Exact descriptor clause evidence and broader Object/Array algorithms remain open. The formerly failing CommonJS wrapper namespace/default-function regression now uses canonical host-format source records and ordinary export owners; complete host interoperability remains open.

Use the matrix as follows:

1. Update the ECMA-262 and Test262 full-SHA pins only as a deliberate baseline change. Run `bun run compliance:fetch`, regenerate the clause catalog with `bun run compliance:catalog`, regenerate the feature inventory with `bun run compliance:features`, and review the normative clause, exact xref/definition, specification-choice, Annex D host-layering, atomic normative-optional-family, feature-tag, proposal-exclusion, and corpus changes. Never use a branch, tag, `latest`, or a pass count as the denominator.
2. Run `bun run compliance:catalog:check`, `bun run compliance:features:check`, `bun run compliance:self-test`, and `bun run compliance:matrix`. The catalog extractor must agree with the independent HTML clause set; Test262 discovery must agree byte-for-byte with the pinned Git tree.
3. Select one unresolved semantic partition, not a fixture count. For every terminal partition, first identify or implement one canonical collection, tree, graph, or worklist lowering; add a compact property test over the semantic partitions and one separate representative stress case. Register every property, stress, E2E, diagnostic, declaration, and implementation artifact in `evidence-registry.json`, and make its exact `clause#partition` targets agree reciprocally with the matrix. A claim-bearing stress registration must name one exact generated positive native-runtime case; its reviewed high-depth value must equal the numeric parameter independently parsed from that case's tracked `generate.json`, so prose cannot promote an ordinary or emit-only fixture into cardinality evidence. The registration must also state the cardinality dimension, rationale, and reviewer. Implementation evidence must name a real symbol locator in a tracked compiler/runtime source file. Existing generated cases remain regression/stress evidence and are not property proof. An authored `finite` label never relaxes these obligations.
4. Map standard feature tags to pinned normative clause IDs in `feature-map.json`. Map current normative `esid` values through the generated anchor catalog. Repair legacy metadata with one reviewed `esidAliases`, non-overlapping `legacyIdRules` exact ID or metadata-section prefix, or semantic-directory `pathRules` entry in `mapping-overrides.json`; qualify a directory rule by `esid` when pinned upstream metadata points at informative material. Match legacy IDs against their declared metadata field, never filenames: an exact rule must equal the complete ID, while a prefix rule must end at a semantic boundary and must not overlap any exact or prefix sibling. Use a source-hashed exact test override only for genuine ambiguity; it may add the specific normative owner while retaining an independently mapped generic normative `esid`, but must never erase that metadata. Never create a file-by-file fixture ladder, infer mappings invisibly, use globs, or leave an unused mapping rule.
5. Add an exact `clauseEvidence` row for every normative clause, including clauses in the descriptive top-level groups. Give every semantic partition its own Test262 query, structurally validated `coveredBy` edge, or exact `tc39/test262` issue plus local executable evidence for an upstream gap. Every terminal partition requires a canonical cardinality mechanism, a registered property specification, a distinct representative stress case, and implementation evidence even if an author labels its surface finite; authored cardinality labels never relax proof obligations. `coveredBy` may discharge only a partition reciprocally bound to one mechanically extracted normative definition record, and every edge must name the exact mechanically extracted normative specification xref that proves its direction; note, example, and informative xrefs cannot discharge coverage. Intrinsic partitions also require declaration-surface and generated-C/runtime evidence. Fill the top-level `choiceSelections` worklist exactly: the catalog mechanically derives every implementation-defined, implementation-approximated, and host-defined obligation, including Annex D host-layering entries, and each concrete selection must cite direct terminal implementation evidence for every normative subject. There is no authored “not a choice” escape. The claim profile implements all normative-optional material: every extracted clause, HTML fragment, and complete indentation-scoped algorithm step is mechanically partitioned into one feature family, and each `normativeOptionalEvidence` entry must select `implemented-all` and cite direct partitions that reciprocally name the family and all of its sites across every affected clause. Exclusions and mixed family states are forbidden. A tracking string cannot mark support.
6. During development, run a targeted inventory or `bun run test:test262 -- --allow-ineligible-host --filter <path-fragment>` to diagnose the selected partition. This mode is explicitly diagnostic and non-claimable; targeted results never advance the global claim. Every positive scenario and every exact strict/sloppy/module/raw variant must eventually pass; negative tests pass only at the declared `parse`, `resolution`, or `runtime` phase with the exact error constructor. Unsupported diagnostics, skips, timeouts, crashes, missing harness hooks, and infrastructure errors remain blockers.
7. Finish every still-disabled native Test262 capability in `host-profile.json`. The canonical ordered-root compiler path already preserves raw source bytes, evaluates binder-isolated setup/test/eval Scripts against one runtime Global Environment Record, atomically instantiates their lexical/function/`var` declaration collections, initializes every global BindingName through one recursive binding tree with iterator closing and object-rest exclusion, returns ScriptEvaluation values through one UpdateEmpty completion slot, emits runner-owned native artifacts, captures host `print`, waits for the async completion protocol, reports trapped setup/test/module/event-loop failures, classifies canonical static ModuleRequest import-attribute/JSON linking, separates graph-wide declaration instantiation from source-ordered synchronous cyclic Module evaluation, evaluates runtime-shaped finite-AOT ImportCall options/attributes, and derives finite-AOT `$262.evalScript`, direct-`eval`, and indirect-`eval` records from one transitive exact-source worklist without delegating semantics. Direct and indirect eval keep distinct generated PerformEval dispatchers: both guard `%eval%`, preserve non-String and completion identity, isolate strict declarations, and use configurable sloppy global function/`var` bindings; indirect calls additionally follow one conservative eval-identity value-flow graph and execute in the eval function's Realm. The reviewed `host-$262-evalScript` capability is enabled; sources outside the statically proven exact-source graph still fail closed and do not delegate. Complete PerformEval remains actionable work: finish class/private/super/with environments, sloppy eval Annex B insertion and catch redeclaration exceptions, and complete source precision for object coercion, mutable iterator Lists, caller-dependent eval results and runtime source sets that cannot be proved finite. Caller function/catch/block bindings, fresh declarative records and retained ordinary/resumable closure capture now use the shared Environment Record tree and cell aliases described above. The new canonical Realm context already isolates global objects, global lexical bindings, Realm-bound host records, normal evaluation, and abrupt restoration; finish `$262.createRealm` by moving every intrinsic constructor/prototype/subsystem singleton and callable `[[Realm]]` into that context, then prove cross-Realm default-prototype, error-origin, well-known-symbol, function-call/construct, and host-evaluation partitions before enabling the flag. The host deliberately remains claim-ineligible until asynchronous cyclic Module evaluation and top-level await, non-finite dynamic host loading and the remaining ImportCall suspension/object-model partitions, the remaining strict early-error and resolution partitions, `[[CanBlock]]`, the remaining Realm intrinsic partitions, and the rest of the pinned `$262` surface are implemented and independently tested. Use protocol 4 and the `tsc2c-runner-owned-native-v1` contract: the host may only prepare a complete artifact worklist inside the ephemeral runner-owned directory. The runner independently walks and hashes the exact generated-C/native-or-diagnostics artifacts, rejects links/extras/path escapes and delegated engine artifacts, runs the preparer beneath the child-subreaper worklist supervisor, seals the worklist, and launches the dynamic Linux native executable beneath the independently rebuilt seccomp guard. The guard handshake, kernel state, supervisor/guard identities, post-run artifact hashes, request, compiler/runtime implementation, dependency lock, host executable, and sanitized environment all enter the runner-authored transcript/report. A surviving descendant, unguarded/static executable, executable-memory/process-spawn attempt, setup-script error, or async-completion failure fails closed and cannot satisfy expected test-source metadata. Tested ECMAScript semantics must execute in generated C/runtime code; embedded Node, `--unsafe-eval`, or another reference engine is forbidden as conformance evidence.
   Realm migration status: function identities now carry and enter their creation Realm; Object, Function, Array, primitive-wrapper, Date, RegExp, native-error, Math, JSON, Reflect, Proxy, and the descriptor-driven global numeric/URI function set are Realm-owned; finite indirect eval enters the eval function's Realm; and explicit constructor metadata drives both intrinsic-default prototype selection and exotic no-receiver construction. Reflective `Array.from` and `Array.of` now feed generic constructor receivers through that shared construction path, so non-object prototypes fall back in the constructor's Realm; their exact clause partitions bind independent property and item-worklist stress evidence, and both complete pinned clause queries are clean in targeted diagnostic mode. This does not substitute for the exhaustive signed report. `%Reflect%` is now one ordinary Realm-owned global value installed from a canonical descriptor collection; its parent surface and `Reflect.construct` have reciprocal property/stress/declaration/implementation evidence. Construct target/newTarget validation precedes one array-like-to-List worklist consumed by ordinary, bound, and Proxy construction, including a cardinality-independent typed-rest slice. The pinned construct slice is clean in targeted diagnostic mode, but unresolved ArrowFunction child clauses, the remaining Reflect child operations, and global report gates stay open. Array prototype well-known-symbol descriptors now live on the creation Realm's actual prototype object and flow through the ordinary PropertyKey/descriptor/own-key algorithms; do not reintroduce caller-Realm virtual intrinsic slots. `%Proxy%` has no own `prototype`, consumes the canonical runtime argument array, exposes an ordinary Realm-owned `revocable` method, and reports native `TypeError` objects across construction, revocation, trap-validation, and invariant failures. Global `parseInt` and `parseFloat` use arbitrary-length source scans; the shared `ToNumber` path implements the complete StringNumericLiteral grammar for `Number`, unary `+`, `isFinite`, and `isNaN`, and its exact String-input parent clause now has reciprocal property/stress/implementation evidence plus a reviewed legacy metadata mapping. URI Encode/Decode share a strict scalar codec over the runtime's surrogate-preserving WTF-8 representation, and each Realm-owned `String` constructor materializes ordinary mutable `fromCharCode`, `fromCodePoint`, and `raw` built-ins. `String.fromCharCode` and `String.fromCodePoint` now bind their exact intrinsic clauses to the shared runtime argument/materialization worklists, one independent property, and distinct representative stresses. Every pinned `fromCodePoint` scenario executes natively and its own feature/legacy metadata is mapped, but the Symbol-tagged scenario still depends on the separate unmapped `Symbol` feature; targeted results are not a claim. The numeric and Proxy constructors consume arbitrary runtime argument worklists with ordered conversion/evaluation rather than variadic fixed-shape ABIs, while the shared dynamic-call path performs method lookup before evaluating arguments. String primitive/boxed length, indexed values/descriptors, own-key enumeration, reflection, copies, equality/order/hash, search, range extraction, padding, character/code-point access, iteration, and well-formedness now consume one lazily published immutable UTF-16 code-unit collection over WTF-8 storage. Literal replace/replaceAll/string-separator split now use the same collection for non-overlapping matching, replacement-token ranges, empty-search insertion, empty-separator unit extraction, and limits; their byte-offset string-substitution predecessor is deleted. `replace`, `replaceAll`, and `split` now also occupy the ordinary String prototype through one descriptor collection. Typed and dynamic calls resolve that mutable property before arguments, then one shared generic path performs `RequireObjectCoercible`, object-only `Symbol.replace`/`Symbol.split` dispatch, ordered coercions, omitted-argument handling, `replaceAll`'s RegExp/global guard, and functional literal replacement over one precomputed match-position worklist. Independent dual-memory properties prove the generic semantic partitions and equivalent scalar/explicit-surrogate storage, while the one independently derived representative stress guards both the shared sequence and functional boundary worklist at width. This does not expand reciprocal exact evidence beyond String exotic `[[GetOwnProperty]]` and `String.prototype.at`: complete RegExp `@@replace`/`@@split`, captures/named captures, UTF-16 RegExp offsets and zero-width advancement, pinned exact queries, and the corresponding clause rows remain open. Remaining exact clause evidence also includes the mechanically defensible relationships for Proxy internal methods, URI abstract operations, `String.raw`, the StringNumericValue/RoundMVResult child clauses, the remaining String exotic internal methods, and the wider UTF-16 String method surface. The native RegExp consumers now share a UTF-16 pattern tree, immutable continuation graph, normalized character sets, and capture spans. Stateful `lastIndex`, ordinary mutable `exec`/`test`/`match`/`search`, result metadata/indices, scoped modifiers, disjoint duplicate names, and ordinary constructor call/clone/reflective Realm semantics now use shared records and runtime operations. Source serialization and UTF-16 `escape` now share the canonical sequence/output collection, named ordinary accessors, and independent round-trip evidence. RegExp `@@replace` now uses the shared execution/result/capture Lists and the same `GetSubstitution` as literal String replacement. Finish split/matchAll symbol protocols and species/iterator behavior, remaining syntax and Annex B partitions, reciprocal exact RegExp/String clause evidence, and full intrinsic Realm migration; also finish normalization/case conversion, trim/repeat/concatenation normalization, any remaining byte-oriented helper, and the ordinary generic surface for the other String intrinsics. Continue by moving every remaining constructor/prototype and intrinsic-owned mutable subsystem through the same key-addressed Realm collection, not by adding constructor-test families. Extend the independent property partition plan for each newly migrated semantic category, retain one representative Realm-state worklist stress, and keep `host-$262-createRealm` disabled until the complete intrinsic registry, remaining finite host-evaluation partitions, cross-Realm call/construct/error/default-prototype behavior, and pinned targeted probes all pass.

The method performs `RequireObjectCoercible` with receiver-first `ToString` (including exact abrupt identity), snapshots the canonical char sequence, and returns a fresh iterator per call; the shared prototype's `next` drives each iterator through one index worklist with `{value, done}` results, rejects borrowed receivers with a real `TypeError`, and returns the receiver from its own `@@iterator`. One independent property derives the shared semantic plan, while `string_utf16_exotic_width` remains the single representative sequence-width stress. Separate exact clause rows, a semantic-directory mapping (legacy es6id directory), exact-esid metadata, a source-hashed constructability override, and reciprocal artifacts bind the intrinsic, and the complete pinned Symbol.iterator directory is clean in targeted diagnostic mode. Ordinary synchronous for-of and literal spread now observe actual iterator/next lookup through the shared iterator record. Remaining native collection adapters and resumable consumers are separate iteration-protocol partitions. Do not infer `Array.prototype.values`, generator iterators, or any other String clause from this closed family.

   String-raw update: `String.raw` keeps its ordinary constructor descriptor and one generic template worklist over the canonical UTF-16 sequence, now with spec-exact conversion. The operation performs template and raw `ToObject` (real `TypeError` on nullish either), `LengthOfArrayLike` via `ToNumber` (replacing a bit-cast length), ordered segment-then-substitution conversion with missing substitutions skipped and extras ignored, and exact abrupt identity throughout. The compile-time tag fold consumes the same raw parts with engine CR/CRLF-to-LF normalization. One independent property derives the shared semantic plan. Separate exact clause rows, a semantic-directory mapping (legacy es6id directory), a source-hashed constructability override, and reciprocal artifacts bind the intrinsic, and the complete pinned String/raw directory (30 files) is clean in targeted diagnostic mode. Do not infer `String.fromCharCode`/`fromCodePoint`, template-tagged `String()` calls, or any other String clause from this closed family.

   Feature-classification update: the broad BigInt, `Symbol.match`, `Symbol.toPrimitive`, and computed-property-name Test262 tags now map to their shared language-type/object, well-known-Symbol, or syntax parent dependencies. This removes ambiguous dependency classification only. Exact esid/production metadata and operation-specific tags must still bind every constructor, prototype, conversion, numeric, class/object, RegExp, and consumer terminal partition; these broad mappings are not support claims.

8. After dependency installation, run `bun run prepare:e2e-fixtures` to restore the deterministic tracked package fixtures, and verify the source tree is clean. The release profile requires libdispatch at the reviewed Swift prefix `/usr/share/swift/usr`; its header and library identities are recorded, and the conformance E2E gates use `--fail-on-skip`. From that one exact commit, run the exhaustive pinned Test262 worklist (merge all shards by exact scenario-set equality) and the unchanged build, emitter-reachability, GC E2E, no-GC E2E, generated regression, property, and representative stress gates. Sharding is resource plumbing, not completion evidence.
9. Run `bun run compliance:claim` with the exhaustive Test262 and local-gate reports. Publish the generated wording only when it exits zero and the release workflow has signed the final claim. The gate rejects incomplete mappings, unresolved partitions, dirty/stale source, internally inconsistent report digests, host/profile drift, non-pinned corpus bytes, semantic delegation, non-passing outcomes, missing local evidence, and any evidence report without a valid GitHub artifact-attestation bundle bound to the exact repository, release workflow, source revision, and GitHub-hosted runner policy. Every shard/gate report is signed before transport; the merge job verifies those signatures, signs both merged reports, passes their bundles into the claim checker, and signs the resulting claim. Never substitute unsigned local JSON, percentages, aggregate pass totals, or prose over fixture families for this gate.

The manual **ECMAScript 2026 conformance evidence** workflow is the release path. Dispatch it for the exact revision under review. Its preflight intentionally fails red and uploads `ecmascript-2026-claim-<source-sha>` while the functional native Test262 host remains claim-ineligible; terminal partitions without reciprocally registered property specifications, unresolved clause/choice/optional partitions, and any unavailable required E2E dependency remain explicit local-gate blockers. The property runner is non-empty and enforces architectural async-CFG invariants, but that specification cannot discharge an ECMAScript clause until the exact terminal partition also binds distinct reviewed stress and implementation evidence. Only a reviewed host with `claimEligible: true`, no semantic delegation, and every required capability enabled proceeds to exhaustive shards, exact merges, and the final claim job.

When a partition is completed, remove its shared implementation gap, regenerate the checklist, and move historical implementation notes to [`done.md`](done.md). Do not check boxes by hand.

## Engineering acceptance rules

These are mandatory acceptance criteria for new work, not backlog items. An exception requires a documented semantic or performance reason, a named limit where applicable, and regression coverage that would fail if the exception were accidentally broadened.

### Design and coding style

- Model arbitrary language cardinality with collections, trees, graphs, or worklists. Never add numbered semantic fields or paths such as `second` / `third` / `fourth`, one handler per branch count, or one implementation extension per await/yield/argument count.
- Keep one canonical representation and one lowering path for each semantic concept. A generalized implementation must replace and delete its fixed-shape predecessors; do not retain a generic path plus legacy count-specific fallbacks.
- Any real implementation bound must be named, centralized, justified by a platform or resource constraint, diagnosed when exceeded, and tested immediately below, at, and above the boundary. Accidental AST-shape or recursion limits are bugs.
- Separate recognition, semantic analysis, lowering, and C emission. Normalize related source shapes into a shared typed plan, IR, or control-flow graph before emission instead of growing source-shape templates in the emitter.
- Use TypeScript checker symbols, signatures, and types for identity and semantics whenever available. Do not infer binding identity, overload choice, or runtime behavior from identifier text or printer output.
- Prefer typed dispatch tables and small strategy objects over copy-pasted `if` chains. Adding a sibling operation should normally add one table entry or strategy, not another duplicated traversal.
- Keep modules and functions cohesive. New feature families belong in focused analysis/lowering/runtime modules; do not make an existing god emitter or resolver larger without recording why extraction is unsafe and leaving the touched area smaller or more structured.
- Centralize shared invariants, coercions, type mappings, name generation, ownership rules, and diagnostics. Emitter and runtime implementations must not independently redefine the same language rule.
- Do not silence type errors, C warnings, or unreachable-state checks to accommodate generated size or control-flow complexity. Refactor the representation; any narrowly unavoidable suppression must state its invariant and have a regression test.
- Tests must be organized by semantic partition and interaction, not by successive integer counts. Generate high-depth/property stress inputs from compact declarative specs, and keep one representative boundary test rather than hand-checking thousands of repetitive source lines into the repository.
- Test infrastructure must be hermetic and scope-aware: a filtered test must not rewrite unrelated fixtures or the working tree, generated artifacts belong in temporary/build directories, and test order must not affect results.
- A feature change is incomplete until its declaration surface, checker/resolution logic, IR/lowering, runtime/ABI support, positive and negative tests, and relevant documentation agree.
- Keep this file limited to actionable remaining work. Move completed implementation notes and verification records to [`done.md`](done.md) in the same change.

### TypeScript-to-C transpiler correctness

- Preserve observable ECMAScript behavior before optimizing: left-to-right evaluation, single evaluation of side effects, short-circuiting, coercion, lexical scope/TDZ/hoisting, property and prototype semantics, exceptions/finally completion, iterator closing, and async microtask ordering.
- Treat TypeScript types as compile-time evidence, not runtime truth. Erasure, `any`/`unknown`, unions, assertions, JavaScript inputs, and external modules must use sound runtime representations or an explicit unsupported diagnostic; never silently specialize away possible runtime values.
- Lower control flow through explicit basic blocks/states and completion edges. `return`, `throw`, `break`, `continue`, `yield`, and `await` must compose through nesting without adding syntax-depth-specific emitters.
- Async lowering must be a general state machine with explicit live-local capture, Promise/thenable adoption, rejection propagation, `finally` routing, and specified job ordering. No continuation may retain a pointer to expired C stack storage.
- Iterator and generator lowering must implement acquisition, `next`, abrupt completion, `return`/close, delegation, and sync/async protocol distinctions on every exit edge.
- Module lowering must preserve resolution conditions, one-time initialization, live bindings, re-exports, cycles, CommonJS interop, and deterministic dependency order. Dynamic loading/evaluation requires an explicit finite AOT proof or manifest rather than guessing.
- Make ownership and lifetime visible at every TypeScript-to-C boundary: allocation owner, GC root, borrowed/owned value, captured environment, native handle, cleanup action, and transfer point. All normal and abrupt exits must release or retain resources correctly.
- Emit portable, defined C for the supported compiler/standard matrix. Avoid signed overflow, invalid shifts, aliasing violations, unsequenced side effects, uninitialized reads, dangling pointers, and layout assumptions; use explicit-width types and checked conversions where JavaScript and C numeric semantics differ.
- Keep the generated-code ABI versioned and centralized. Runtime helpers must document argument/result ownership and exception behavior, and emitter/runtime changes that cross the ABI boundary must land and test together.
- Unsupported semantics must fail closed with a stable, source-located diagnostic. Never emit plausible C for a case whose behavior the compiler has not proved it can preserve.
- Generated C must be deterministic, hygienically named, inspectable, and traceable to TypeScript source locations. Host paths, hash-map iteration order, and unrelated build state must not perturb output.
- Differentially test observable behavior against the supported Node/JavaScript/TypeScript reference, including stdout, stderr, exit status, thrown values, ordering, and side-effect counts. Add negative-diagnostic, module-cycle, GC/lifetime, deep-control-flow, and cross-feature tests as appropriate.
- Compile generated C under the supported GCC/Clang warning profile and exercise sanitizers, leak/GC modes, optimization levels, and platform CI where practical. A transpiler test does not end when C text is produced.
- Optimize only after semantic equivalence is established. Every fast path needs a proven guard, a general semantic fallback or explicit diagnostic, and differential tests that force both paths.
- Treat source text, module specifiers, manifests, generated identifiers/literals, native addon paths, and runtime-code inputs as untrusted. Validate ranges and paths, escape generated C data, and keep dynamic capabilities allow-listed and reproducible.
